Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in hamiltonian theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 936 and the invariant I(A,B) = 242 mod 5.

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 242. QED.

Theorem 219.5 (Spectral Property)
